appointment frame."},{"label":"medium","ref":"82:17","note":"وما أدراك ما يوم الدين reinforces the day-of-reckoning frame in context."},{"label":"medium","ref":"83:4","note":"مبعوثون ليوم عظيم gives a collective day-of-emergence counterpart."}],"channel_subchannels_anchored_here":[{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Debt, Promise, and the Appointed Settlement","parent_semantic_invariant":"A future claim binds the present through debt, truthful commitment, trust, and a fixed time of encounter.","parent_surprising_reach":"Eschatological promise becomes a ledger of loan, entitlement, mutual undertaking, due date, and collection.","key":"B","name":"Trust and reciprocal undertaking","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A promise enters custody, becomes a covenant between parties, and awaits faithful performance.","active_motifs":"entrusted security `quranic:root_000054:B001/m01`; heart-settled belief `quranic:root_000054:B002/m01`; protected covenant `quranic:root_001055:B003/m01`; commitment opening expectation `quranic:root_001662:B001/m01`; fixed time or place of promise `quranic:root_001662:B003/m01`; reciprocal promising `quranic:root_001662:B004/m01`; performed commitment `quranic:root_000852:B004/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:26` يُصَدِّقُون (`ص د ق`); `70:28,32` مَأْمُون/أَمَانَات (`ء م ن`); `70:32` عَهْد (`ع ه د`); `70:42,44` يُوعَدُون (`و ع د`).","synthesis":"Promise is not only information about the future. It is deposited as trust, ratified as covenant, shared between parties, and located at a due time. The repeated promised day is the date on which custody turns into performance.","ayah_refs":["70:26","70:28","70:32","70:42"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Debt, Promise, and the Appointed Settlement","parent_semantic_invariant":"A future claim binds the present through debt, truthful commitment, trust, and a fixed time of encounter.","parent_surprising_reach":"Eschatological promise becomes a ledger of loan, entitlement, mutual undertaking, due date, and collection.","key":"C","name":"Threat arriving at its appointment","reading_type":"surface-primary","scene_or_process":"Warning signs intensify until the addressee meets the harmful event at its stated time.","active_motifs":"threatening warning `quranic:root_001662:B002/m01`; stallion's warning before attack `quranic:root_001662:B005/m01`; signs promising a coming condition `quranic:root_001662:B006/m01`; face-to-face encounter `quranic:root_001372:B004/m01`; experienced adversity `quranic:root_001372:B007/m01`; catastrophic day `quranic:root_001700:B003/m01`; punitive pain `quranic:root_000994:B005/m01`; sovereign owner `quranic:root_000532:B001/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:27-28` عَذَاب رَبِّهِم (`ع ذ ب`, `ر ب ب`); `70:42` يُلَاقُوا يَوْمَهُم الَّذِي يُوعَدُون (`ل ق ي`, `ي و م`, `و ع د`); `70:44` الْيَوْم الَّذِي كَانُوا يُوعَدُون (`ي و م`, `و ع د`).","synthesis":"Threat develops like an animal warning before charge: a signal, a voiced menace, advancing harm, then encounter. The promised day is not merely reached on a calendar; it meets the person as an experienced event.","ayah_refs":["70:27","70:42","70:44"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Witness, Knowledge, and Truth Made Public","parent_semantic_invariant":"Private perception becomes reliable public evidence by being remembered, marked, spoken, and embodied in a standing witness.","parent_surprising_reach":"Evidence includes bodily presence, tongue, landmark, memory, eye-sign, truthful performance, and exposure of fabricated interiors.","key":"C","name":"Truthful performance versus fabricated interior","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Speech is tested against action and inward condition; invention and concealed corruption fail that test.","active_motifs":"truthful utterance `quranic:root_000852:B001/m01`; complete, sound condition `quranic:root_000852:B003/m01`; promise realized in action `quranic:root_000852:B004/m01`; fabricated discourse `quranic:root_000434:B007/m01`; hidden interior `quranic:root_000464:B003/m01`; concealed corruption `quranic:root_000464:B004/m01`; receiving and learning words `quranic:root_001372:B011/m01`; rhetorical concealment behind another meaning `quranic:root_001642:B005/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:19,39` خُلِق/خَلَقْنَاهُم (`خ ل ق`); `70:26` يُصَدِّقُون (`ص د ق`); `70:31` وَرَاء (`و ر ي`); `70:38` يُدْخَل (`د خ ل`); `70:42` يُلَاقُوا (`ل ق ي`).","synthesis":"Truth is correspondence carried into performance. Fabrication, hidden interior, and rhetorical cover separate surface utterance from inward state, while testimony reunites what is said, known, and done.","ayah_refs":["70:19","70:26","70:31","70:38","70:42"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Coveted Ingress, Decoy, and Trap","parent_semantic_invariant":"Desire for an interior can be manipulated by a visible lure, converting self-directed approach into capture.","parent_surprising_reach":"Garden entry becomes a hunting scene with decoy bird, set snare, raised marker, game, and protective screen.","key":"B","name":"Lure, snare, and raised target","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A decoy draws movement into a set apparatus centered on a visible upright object.","active_motifs":"decoy bird inside a net `quranic:root_000951:B005/m01`; set hunting snare `quranic:root_000879:B004/m01`; erected object or trap `quranic:root_001507:B001/m01`; cultic stone drawing approach `quranic:root_001507:B002/m01`; boundary or trough marker `quranic:root_001507:B003/m01`; guessing game about a concealed hand `quranic:root_000400:B010/m01`; upright tool or support `quranic:root_001273:B012/m01`; named object shaped by a bend `quranic:root_001372:B002/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:22-23,34` صَلَاة (`ص ل و`); `70:33` قَائِمُون (`ق و م`); `70:38` يَطْمَع (`ط م ع`); `70:42` يُلَاقُوا (`ل ق ي`); `70:43` يَخْرُجُون، نُصُب (`خ ر ج`, `ن ص ب`).","synthesis":"The desired object can function as a lure rather than a reward. Decoy, snare, upright support, and marker organize movement toward a center, while the hidden-hand game mirrors ignorance about what the apparent goal actually contains.","ayah_refs":["70:22","70:33","70:38","70:42","70:43"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Horizon Oath and Sovereign Replacement","parent_semantic_invariant":"Rule over recurring horizons establishes the capacity to redesign and replace a population before it can escape.","parent_surprising_reach":"Cosmic horizon, legal division, measured design, proxy, changed form, and footrace are one executable proof.","key":"A","name":"Recurrent eastings and westings","reading_type":"surface-primary","scene_or_process":"Light rises and sets across multiple horizon points, making daily recurrence a visible domain of rule.","active_motifs":"rising light and eastern horizon `quranic:root_000790:B001/m01`; sunlit place of prayer `quranic:root_000790:B006/m01`; setting sun and western horizon `quranic:root_001077:B006/m01`; exile into distance `quranic:root_001077:B007/m01`; rare or unfamiliar object `quranic:root_001077:B008/m01`; unguided projectile from an unknown source `quranic:root_001077:B013/m01`; far-flying portent `quranic:root_001077:B014/m01`; bounded day `quranic:root_001700:B001/m01`; temporal duration `quranic:root_001700:B002/m01`; twilight `quranic:root_000803:B002/m01`; sinking celestial body `quranic:root_000412:B003/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:27` مُشْفِقُون (`ش ف ق`); `70:40` الْمَشَارِق وَالْمَغَارِب (`ش ر ق`, `غ ر ب`); `70:42-44` يَوْم (`ي و م`); `70:44` خَاشِعَة (`خ ش ع`).","synthesis":"Plural horizons make recurrence spatially visible: each day rises and sets at a shifted point. Exile, unknown-source arrow, and distant portent extend the horizon into domains from which events appear unexpectedly, yet all remain inside the same rule.","ayah_refs":["70:27","70:40","70:42","70:44"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Wading Play Dissolved into Encounter","parent_semantic_invariant":"Aimless activity immerses its participants in a mixed medium until the promised event interrupts and gathers the scattered motion.","parent_surprising_reach":"Futile discourse becomes water-crossing, stirred drink, saliva, venom, wind-erased ground, discarded object, and joined endpoints.","key":"A","name":"Immersion and mixed discourse","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Participants enter a medium, stir its components, and lose distinction between serious action and idle talk.","active_motifs":"entering and crossing water `quranic:root_000446:B001/m01`; plunging into discourse or affair `quranic:root_000446:B002/m01`; stirring liquid with a tool `quranic:root_000446:B003/m01`; interloper entering an affair `quranic:root_000464:B005/m01`; interlocking parts `quranic:root_000464:B008/m01`; confused mixture `quranic:root_001423:B009/m01`; purposeless play `quranic:root_001358:B001/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:20-21` مَسَّه (`م س س`); `70:38` يُدْخَل (`د خ ل`); `70:42` يَخُوضُوا وَيَلْعَبُوا (`خ و ض`, `ل ع ب`).","synthesis":"Wading unifies physical and discursive immersion: one enters, stirs, interlocks, and becomes part of a confused medium. Play is the absence of a sound objective inside that activity, not absence of energetic motion.","ayah_refs":["70:20","70:38","70:42"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Wading Play Dissolved into Encounter","parent_semantic_invariant":"Aimless activity immerses its participants in a mixed medium until the promised event interrupts and gathers the scattered motion.","parent_surprising_reach":"Futile discourse becomes water-crossing, stirred drink, saliva, venom, wind-erased ground, discarded object, and joined endpoints.","key":"B","name":"Sweetness, saliva, and venom","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Fluids issuing from mouths or animals can nourish, intoxicate, or poison, making surface liquidity morally ambiguous.","active_motifs":"flowing saliva `quranic:root_001358:B002/m01`; honey or venom as animal secretion `quranic:root_001358:B003/m01`; honey retained in comb `quranic:root_000822:B007/m01`; wine covering the mind `quranic:root_000819:B009/m01`; fermented drink `quranic:root_001077:B016/m01`; thick syrup used to preserve or season `quranic:root_000532:B006/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:27-28,40` رَب (`ر ب ب`); `70:33` شَهَادَات (`ش ه د`); `70:37` الشِّمَال (`ش م ل`); `70:40` مَغَارِب (`غ ر ب`); `70:42` يَلْعَبُوا (`ل ع ب`).","synthesis":"The same class of issuing fluid ranges from nourishing honey to intoxicant and venom. This instability clarifies why immersion cannot be judged by pleasant surface flow; its source and effect determine whether it feeds, veils, or kills.","ayah_refs":["70:27","70:33","70:37","70:40","70:42"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Wading Play Dissolved into Encounter","parent_semantic_invariant":"Aimless activity immerses its participants in a mixed medium until the promised event interrupts and gathers the scattered motion.","parent_surprising_reach":"Futile discourse becomes water-crossing, stirred drink, saliva, venom, wind-erased ground, discarded object, and joined endpoints.","key":"C","name":"Leaving ends at a forced meeting","reading_type":"surface-primary","scene_or_process":"Persons are temporarily left in motion, but discarded states and separated endpoints are gathered into one encounter.","active_motifs":"leaving or abandoning `quranic:root_001638:B003/m01`; face-to-face meeting `quranic:root_001372:B004/m01`; throwing or casting `quranic:root_001372:B005/m01`; discarded object `quranic:root_001372:B006/m01`; experienced adversity `quranic:root_001372:B007/m01`; lying on the back `quranic:root_001372:B009/m01`; joining two endpoints `quranic:root_001372:B010/m01`; receiving a communicated word `quranic:root_001372:B011/m01`; fixed appointment `quranic:root_001662:B003/m01`; demonstrative pointing `quranic:root_000527:B003/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:42` ذَرْهُم، يُلَاقُوا، يُوعَدُون (`و ذ ر`, `ل ق ي`, `و ع د`); surface anchor unavailable for `ذ و و` in `70:44`.","synthesis":"“Leave them” is temporary permission, not an open destination. Casting, discard, and prone collapse describe the states reached along the way; joining endpoints and meeting show how the promised appointment closes the interval.","ayah_refs":["70:42","70:44"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Wading Play Dissolved into Encounter","parent_semantic_invariant":"Aimless activity immerses its participants in a mixed medium until the promised event interrupts and gathers the scattered motion.","parent_surprising_reach":"Futile discourse becomes water-crossing, stirred drink, saliva, venom, wind-erased ground, discarded object, and joined endpoints.","key":"D","name":"Wind erases the playground","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Wind moves through an exposed place until prior tracks and structures are effaced.","active_motifs":"wind wearing away a dwelling `quranic:root_001358:B005/m01`; broad darkness or water that covers `quranic:root_001307:B002/m01`; north wind and its cooling action `quranic:root_000819:B007/m01`; soft south wind `quranic:root_001525:B009/m01`; western direction and setting `quranic:root_001077:B006/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:36` كَفَرُوا (`ك ف ر`); `70:37` الشِّمَال (`ش م ل`); `70:38` نَعِيم (`ن ع م`); `70:40` مَغَارِب (`غ ر ب`); `70:42` يَلْعَبُوا (`ل ع ب`).","synthesis":"Play's “place” is not durable. Opposed winds traverse it, darkness covers it, and setting removes the light by which its traces were visible. The scene gives the command to leave them a temporal weathering process.","ayah_refs":["70:36","70:37","70:38","70:40","70:42"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Food, Fire, and the Throat of Processing","parent_semantic_invariant":"Material is transformed by heat, drying, chewing, swallowing, and digestion into nourishment, residue, or injury.","parent_surprising_reach":"Sun-curing, spark, roasting drippings, kindled fire, stirred drink, saliva, choking, esophagus, feast, and intensified grinding form one alimentary mechanism.","key":"A","name":"Mouth, esophagus, and digestible food","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Food is tasted, chewed, lubricated, swallowed through the esophagus, and judged by whether it sits easily.","active_motifs":"pleasant digestible food `quranic:root_001409:B003/m01`; esophagus `quranic:root_001409:B004/m01`; eating and ceremonial feeding `quranic:root_001409:B005/m01`; light digestible food `quranic:root_001410:B003/m01`; eating and feast-giving `quranic:root_001410:B004/m01`; esophageal passage `quranic:root_001410:B005/m01`; softness and refined food `quranic:root_001525:B002/m01`; intensifying an operation such as grinding `quranic:root_001525:B010/m01`; choking obstruction `quranic:root_000790:B005/m01`; flowing saliva `quranic:root_001358:B002/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:38` امْرِئ، نَعِيم (`م ر ء`, `ن ع م`); `70:40` الْمَشَارِق (`ش ر ق`); `70:42` يَلْعَبُوا (`ل ع ب`).","synthesis":"Nourishment requires a continuous passage from mouth to stomach. Softness, saliva, esophagus, and digestibility maintain that route; choking reveals how a small obstruction converts desirable intake into bodily threat.","ayah_refs":["70:38","70:40","70:42"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Food, Fire, and the Throat of Processing","parent_semantic_invariant":"Material is transformed by heat, drying, chewing, swallowing, and digestion into nourishment, residue, or injury.","parent_surprising_reach":"Sun-curing, spark, roasting drippings, kindled fire, stirred drink, saliva, choking, esophagus, feast, and intensified grinding form one alimentary mechanism.","key":"B","name":"Drying, kindling, and rendered drippings","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Food is exposed to sun, cut, heated, and stirred until moisture leaves and fat or blood flows.","active_motifs":"sun-curing meat `quranic:root_000790:B002/m01`; laying food in sun to dry `quranic:root_000787:B002/m01`; flying sparks `quranic:root_000787:B003/m01`; fat dripping from roast meat `quranic:root_000787:B005/m01`; enduring fire and heat `quranic:root_000879:B001/m01`; hidden fire struck from flint `quranic:root_001642:B002/m01`; stirring drink or blood `quranic:root_000446:B003/m01`; cutting meat and incising a wound `quranic:root_001638:B002/m01`; calming a boiling pot with water `quranic:root_000501:B002/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:20` الشَّر (`ش ر ر`); `70:22-23,34` صَلَاة (`ص ل و`); `70:23` دَائِمُون (`د و م`); `70:31` وَرَاء (`و ر ي`); `70:40` الْمَشَارِق (`ش ر ق`); `70:42` يَخُوضُوا، ذَرْهُم (`خ و ض`, `و ذ ر`).","synthesis":"Processing alternates removal and addition: sun removes moisture, cutting opens matter, fire renders fat, stirring mixes fluid, and water quiets boil. The chain clarifies how the same heat can prepare provision or materialize punishment depending on its end.","ayah_refs":["70:20","70:22","70:23","70:31","70:40","70:42"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Food, Fire, and the Throat of Processing","parent_semantic_invariant":"Material is transformed by heat, drying, chewing, swallowing, and digestion into nourishment, residue, or injury.","parent_surprising_reach":"Sun-curing, spark, roasting drippings, kindled fire, stirred drink, saliva, choking, esophagus, feast, and intensified grinding form one alimentary mechanism.","key":"C","name":"Preserved sweetness and intoxicating residue","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Sweet or fermented substances remain held in comb, syrup, or vessel and alter the consumer.","active_motifs":"honey in its wax `quranic:root_000822:B007/m01`; honey or venom as secretion `quranic:root_001358:B003/m01`; wine covering cognition `quranic:root_000819:B009/m01`; fermented date drink `quranic:root_001077:B016/m01`; thick preserving syrup `quranic:root_000532:B006/m01`; intoxicating drink and dizziness `quranic:root_000501:B004/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:23` دَائِمُون (`د و م`); `70:27-28,40` رَب (`ر ب ب`); `70:33` شَهَادَات (`ش ه د`); `70:37` الشِّمَال (`ش م ل`); `70:40` مَغَارِب (`غ ر ب`); `70:42` يَلْعَبُوا (`ل ع ب`).","synthesis":"Preservation can retain sweetness, poison, or intoxicant. Comb and syrup stabilize valuable matter, but wine shows that a preserved liquid can destabilize the person who consumes it.","ayah_refs":["70:23","70:27","70:33","70:37","70:40","70:42"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Wound, Infection, and Bodily Burden","parent_semantic_invariant":"A breached body can swell, suppurate, transmit harm, and lose the alignment needed for free movement.","parent_surprising_reach":"Incision, boil, lung disease, facial palsy, spreading wound, transmitted illness, chest frame, long neck, eye swelling, and overloaded limbs form a clinical scene.","key":"A","name":"Incision, swelling, and suppuration","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Tissue is cut, a wound spreads, pus consumes the interior, and a swelling emerges visibly.","active_motifs":"festering wound `quranic:root_000138:B004/m01`; boil or bodily eruption `quranic:root_000400:B004/m01`; cutting and shredding `quranic:root_000787:B004/m01`; incision in flesh or wound `quranic:root_001638:B002/m01`; lung or internal suppuration `quranic:root_001642:B001/m01`; severe bodily disease `quranic:root_001077:B017/m01`; facial palsy and distortion `quranic:root_001372:B001/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:20` الشَّر (`ش ر ر`); `70:31` ابْتَغَى، وَرَاء (`ب غ ي`, `و ر ي`); `70:40` مَغَارِب (`غ ر ب`); `70:42` يُلَاقُوا، ذَرْهُم (`ل ق ي`, `و ذ ر`); `70:43` يَخْرُجُون (`خ ر ج`).","synthesis":"The wound develops causally: incision opens, tissue swells, corruption travels inward, a boil pushes outward, and distortion appears in the face. ## Your response

Write in tr. Produce your response as three parts, in this order. If you return one combined response, label the parts clearly. If an orchestrator asks you to write the parts into separate files, omit wrapper labels from the prose and evidence files; the file path already supplies the label.

1. **The prose** — per the output contract in `COMMENTARY_SPEC.md` §5 and the task document above: continuous, single voice, no provenance markers, no headers named after evidence layers.
2. **The evidence surface** — separate from the prose, addressable per phrase, per `PRINCIPLES.md` §12.
3. A section headed exactly `=== PROMPT FRICTION ===` reporting honestly where the specification above was unclear, self-contradictory, underspecified, or impossible to follow, and any point where you had to invent a rule to proceed. Report this section every time, even if the run went cleanly — say so explicitly if you found nothing. This is how the specification gets improved.

## V2 Rendering Profile — 5.6 Sol High

This run tests whether `5.6-sol-high` can increase lexical depth while preserving
reader-facing clarity.

Increase depth through semantic precision, not additional bulk.

Before drafting, silently build a coverage ledger:

- include every `must_integrate` topic;
- include every `candidate` with a reader payoff not already expressed;
- if `v12_reader_responses` is absent because the default workflow retired
  per-ayah focus runs, do not infer a `stage_00` isolated response or
  confidence movement;
- if `v12_focus_trace_hermetic` is present, use its `baseline_models`,
  `context_deltas`, and `surprising_valid_outliers` as reconstructed
  before/after evidence. Do not call it a staged reveal transcript. Preserve
  surprising outliers when they remain anchored in this ayah, especially
  secondary split-root activations;
- include a regular reader-walk, plus/minus-5 reader-walk, whole-surah reading,
  or cross-run-publication item only when it adds a distinct retrospective
  insight or coverage check;
- use branch inventories to support explanations; they create no standalone
  prose obligation. When fallback inventories are present, treat them as
  restricted to this ayah's roots and anchored citations, not as a whole-surah
  obligation.
- treat `root_lexicon` as full dictionary/gloss support, not branch-filtered.
  It uses QAC-to-Furuq root mapping; when a QAC root is split, non-dominant
  Furuq targets are included as additional root entries and recorded in
  coverage. If multiple QAC roots map to the same Furuq root, the shared entry
  records all source roots in `qac_roots_ar` / `qac_root_mappings`; use those
  fields for evidence attribution when present, not the legacy single `root_ar`
  alone. Dictionary/gloss branches are evidence support, not independent
  obligations, unless tied to this ayah's word, topic, reader payoff, or cited
  relation. Full field means all distinct reader payoffs from activated
  material, not every dictionary branch for the root.
- if `channel_generated_outputs` lists quran-data files and this run gives you
  file access, read only those listed files when channel-family/path detail is
  necessary. Treat them as candidate/family/path evidence, not as an adjudicated
  channel ledger. State B channel restrictions still apply.

For each critical word, preserve these distinct layers when available:

1. local grammatical work;
2. locally selected sense;
3. coherent pressure supplied by activated or cited root branches;
4. one form, sound, rarity, or variant observation with a unique payoff;
5. later contextual change, compressed to its final reader-visible result.

Do not flatten these layers into a general metaphor. Deepen generic summaries by
naming the exact lexical mechanism. Ordering grammar and local meaning first is
grounding, not truth-ranking.

At first mention of an ayah word, use a structured Arabic surface span:
`{ar:surface_form, tr:Turkish-readable transliteration, gloss:target-language meaning}`.
Use the same full span again when the prose returns to that word after moving to
another word or another paragraph. Inside one short local sequence, after a full
span has just been given, a Turkish label or transliteration is enough. Explain
every technical term in the same sentence. Keep raw roots, root skeletons,
letter-by-letter root transliterations, and branch IDs in the evidence surface.
In prose, attach root discussion to the surface word:
`{ar:ٱلْعَادِيَاتِ, tr:el-âdiyât, gloss:koşup atılanlar} kelimesinin bağlı
olduğu kök alanı...`, not `ʿ-d-v kökü...`.

A paragraph may begin with the ayah's surface, a concrete image, or a
reader-facing claim. It may not begin with a bare grammar label or stacked
abstractions. Prefer "Âyet önce hamdi Allah'a verir; sabit ad cümlesi bu hamdi
yerleşik bir hüküm olarak taşır" over "Bu âyet, tek bir isim cümlesiyle yerleşik
bir hüküm kurar."

For this short ayah, keep at least two-thirds of the prose on its own wording.
Compress all later developments into at most three paragraphs and end with one
plain synthesis paragraph.

Use `v12_cross_run_publication`, if present, only as a compact coverage/priority
check derived from regular and plus/minus-5 reader runs. Do not copy it as prose,
and do not let it override local bundle evidence.

Use an explicit negative predicate only to correct a likely misconception,
protect the primary sense from replacement, or preserve live counter-evidence.
Default ceiling for this ayah: three. Never stack two negatives in one sentence.
During final revision, rewrite all other negatives as positive predication.
Do not drive negation to zero by default: if no explicit negative is needed,
include a profile-specific style audit in friction confirming that there was no
live misconception, replacement risk, or counter-evidence requiring one.

Pass only if every paragraph has one distinct reader payoff and the prose remains
clear with the evidence surface closed.
